def latex_seq(n:int) -> int:
    n = round(n)
    init = [0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8, 9, 10, 12, 16, 17, 24, 25, 26, 27, 28, 31, 33, 36, 37, 43, 44, 49, 50, 52]
